# --- operating-mode state ----------------------------------------------------
0x0080dd4e  DAT  operating_mode          b  ; 0 REST 2 PRECHARGE 3 READY 4 DRIVE 5 SHUTDOWN
0x0080e7f4  DAT  commanded_mode          b  ; mirror source of operating_mode
0x0080d55b  DAT  mode_command            b  ; commanded mode written by handlers
0x0080e5ac  DAT  precharge_master_state  b  ; 0 REST 5 precharge 6 HV-active
0x0080e590  DAT  mode_substate           b  ; mode_dispatcher(0x87b2c) READY-gate VERDICT / drive sub-state: 0 REST 1 WAKE 2 PRECHARGE/request 3 READY 4 DRIVE 5 SHUTDOWN 6 HV-active. In precharge (e5ac==5) the dispatcher runs an ordered gate: 1st=drive_inhibit_latched==0 (else why=1), then dtc_rest/shift_eco/overspeed/gear/data_valid (why 2..0x11); ALL pass -> =3(READY)+return, ANY fail -> why-code + =5(SHUTDOWN). CORRECTION (2026-08-21, live co-sim): co-sim DOES reach READY (=3) and hold ~36 frames; drive_inhibit_latched STAYS 0 and P0562 never confirms (its 9V threshold is the 12V system, not HV). READY hold ends when this gate flips 3->5 (a dtc_rest/shift_eco/overspeed/gear/data_valid input, NOT drive_inhibit). why=0xb here is a READY sub-verdict (falls through to =3), NOT a fail. DRIVE (=4) needs the driver-request path via mode_rest_handler/mode_precharge_seq->mode_drive_handler, not yet driven
0x0080e607  DAT  mode_wake_flag          b  ; set by mode_step, cleared by mode_reset
0x0080d55e  DAT  why_not_ready_code      b  ; 1..0x11 reason code
0x0080c00c  DAT  data_valid              b  ; drive/diag-valid flag

0x0080bc52  DAT  inv0x288_b4_dclink_raw  b  ; 0x288 CAN shadow byte4 = DClink/2, read UNSIGNED (2V/count). ⚠ decode_can_status_a's `char cVar2` typed it SIGNED, which spawned a false "d010 caps at 254V / it's a temperature" analysis; the d010 value path reads it via *(byte*) (unsigned) -> byte4=0xa2 gives 324V. 0xFF = invalid marker. [conf: high]
